# IMPERIA COLD WAR — 1970 focus and legacy mechanic corrections.
# IMPERIA COLD WAR — Atomic Soviet focus construction transactions.
# IMPERIA COLD WAR — Scripted national building-grant integration.
COLDWAR_GRANT_complete_COLDWAR_SOV_em_komsomol_mobilisation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			has_focus_tree = COLDWAR_SOV_1970_focus
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_COLDWAR_SOV_em_komsomol_mobilisation }
			tag = SOV
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_COLDWAR_SOV_em_komsomol_mobilisation
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_any_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_GRANT_ready_COLDWAR_SOV_em_komsomol_mobilisation = yes
			}
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus COLDWAR_SOV_em_komsomol_mobilisation"
			set_temp_variable = {
				COLDWAR_SOV_outlook_id = 1
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				COLDWAR_SOV_outlook_amount = 0.03
			}
			COLDWAR_SOV_change_outlook_popularity = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_dynamic_modifier = {
							modifier = COLDWAR_SOV_gosplan_modifier
						}
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					add_dynamic_modifier = {
						modifier = COLDWAR_SOV_gosplan_modifier
					}
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
				MODIFIER = COLDWAR_SOV_gosplan_modifier
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				COLDWAR_SOV_gosplan_production_speed_infrastructure_factor = 0.05
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SOV_mod_production_speed_infrastructure_factor_tt
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_dynamic_modifier = {
							modifier = COLDWAR_SOV_armed_forces_modifier
						}
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					add_dynamic_modifier = {
						modifier = COLDWAR_SOV_armed_forces_modifier
					}
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
				MODIFIER = COLDWAR_SOV_armed_forces_modifier
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				COLDWAR_SOV_armed_forces_conscription_factor = 0.02
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SOV_mod_conscription_factor_tt
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				party_index = 7
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				party_popularity_increase = 0.03
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				temp_outlook_increase = 0
			}
			change_relative_party_popularity = yes
			set_temp_variable = {
				skip_payment = 1
			}
			COLDWAR_GRANT_native_one_random_infrastructure = yes
			set_temp_variable = {
				skip_payment = 0
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				COLDWAR_SOV_gdp_share = 0.004
			}
			COLDWAR_SOV_pay_gdp_share = yes
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_COLDWAR_SOV_em_komsomol_mobilisation
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_COLDWAR_SOV_em_komsomol_mobilisation
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_GRANT_complete_COLDWAR_SOV_bre_a_flat_for_every_family = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			has_focus_tree = COLDWAR_SOV_1970_focus
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_COLDWAR_SOV_bre_a_flat_for_every_family }
			tag = SOV
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_COLDWAR_SOV_bre_a_flat_for_every_family
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_any_pending
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SOV01_B_total_cost = gdp_total }
multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SOV01_B_total_cost = 0.012 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_GRANT_ready_COLDWAR_SOV_bre_a_flat_for_every_family = yes

				hidden_trigger = { tag = SOV IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes has_focus_tree = COLDWAR_SOV_1970_focus }
				emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
							custom_trigger_tooltip = {
								tooltip = COLDWAR_SOV_tt_gensec_bre
								has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_bre
							}
							hidden_trigger = {
								NOT = {
									has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_COLDWAR_SOV_bre_a_flat_for_every_family
								}
							}
							custom_trigger_tooltip = {
								tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_focus_capacity_COLDWAR_SOV_bre_a_flat_for_every_family_tt
								COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_COLDWAR_SOV_bre_a_flat_for_every_family = yes
							}
				COLDWAR_SOV01_B_capacity_bre_a_flat_for_every_family = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_COLDWAR_SOV_bre_a_flat_for_every_family }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_SOV01_B_total_cost > 0 }
				NOT = { check_variable = { treasury < COLDWAR_SOV01_B_total_cost } }
			}
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus COLDWAR_SOV_bre_a_flat_for_every_family"
			set_temp_variable = {
				skip_payment = 1
			}
			COLDWAR_GRANT_native_one_random_infrastructure = yes
			set_temp_variable = {
				skip_payment = 0
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				COLDWAR_SOV_gdp_share = 0.004
			}
			COLDWAR_SOV_pay_gdp_share = yes
			add_stability = 0.05
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_dynamic_modifier = {
							modifier = COLDWAR_SOV_gosplan_modifier
						}
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					add_dynamic_modifier = {
						modifier = COLDWAR_SOV_gosplan_modifier
					}
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
				MODIFIER = COLDWAR_SOV_gosplan_modifier
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				COLDWAR_SOV_gosplan_production_speed_buildings_factor = 0.05
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SOV_mod_production_speed_buildings_factor_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				COLDWAR_SOV_gosplan_social_cost_multiplier_modifier = 0.02
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SOV_mod_social_cost_multiplier_modifier_tt
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				skip_payment = 1
			}
			COLDWAR_SOV01_B_one_random_industrial_complex = yes
			set_temp_variable = {
				skip_payment = 0
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				COLDWAR_SOV_gdp_share = 0.008
			}
			COLDWAR_SOV_pay_gdp_share = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_dynamic_modifier = {
							modifier = COLDWAR_SOV_bre_programme
						}
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					add_dynamic_modifier = {
						modifier = COLDWAR_SOV_bre_programme
					}
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
				MODIFIER = COLDWAR_SOV_bre_programme
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				COLDWAR_SOV_bre_programme_stability_factor = 0.03
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SOV_mod_stability_factor_tt
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
			}
			add_political_power = 50
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_COLDWAR_SOV_bre_a_flat_for_every_family
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_COLDWAR_SOV_bre_a_flat_for_every_family
		}
	}
}

				tooltip = civilian_industry_tax_modifier_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				COLDWAR_DDR_planned_economy_industrial_capacity_factory = 0.03
				tooltip = industrial_capacity_factory_tt
			}
			newline = yes
			set_temp_variable = {
				skip_payment = 1
			}
			COLDWAR_GRANT_native_two_random_infrastructure = yes
			set_temp_variable = {
				skip_payment = 0
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				treasury_change = {
					value = gdp_total
					multiply = -0.0075
				}
			}
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = {
				treasury_change = {
					value = gdp_total
					multiply = 0.005
				}
			}
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = {
				temp_opinion = -5
			}
			change_labour_unions_opinion = yes
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_COLDWAR_DDR_spd_growth_before_distribution
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_COLDWAR_DDR_spd_growth_before_distribution

	}

COLDWAR_GRANT_complete_GEN_1970_capital_defence_scheme = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			has_focus_tree = mdcw_1970_generic_focus
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_GEN_1970_capital_defence_scheme }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_GEN_1970_capital_defence_scheme
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_any_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_GRANT_ready_GEN_1970_capital_defence_scheme = yes
			}
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus GEN_1970_capital_defence_scheme"
			if = {
				limit = {
					capital_scope = {
						is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
						custom_trigger_tooltip = {
							tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_anti_air_building_1_tt
							check_variable = {
								var = building_level@anti_air_building
								value = 4
								compare = less_than_or_equals
							}
						}
					}
				}
				capital_scope = {
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
					add_building_construction = {
						type = anti_air_building
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = {
					treasury_change = -3.25 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				COLDWAR_GRANT_native_one_anti_air = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							has_dlc = "Gotterdammerung"
							NOT = {
								has_tech = air_defense
							}
						}
						NOT = {
							has_tech = air_defense_non_got
						}
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = GENERIC_capital_defense_scheme_tt
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc = "Gotterdammerung"
				}
				add_tech_bonus = {
					name = GENERIC_capital_defense_scheme
					uses = 1
					bonus = 0.25
					category = CAT_surface_to_air_missiles
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_GEN_1970_capital_defence_scheme
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_GEN_1970_capital_defence_scheme
		}
	}
}

	}

		limit = { has_completed_focus = GEN_1970_build_listening_posts NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_GEN_1970_build_listening_posts } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_GEN_1970_build_listening_posts } }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_GEN_1970_build_listening_posts
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_GEN_1970_wa_the_new_village_movement }
		COLDWAR_GRANT_complete_GEN_1970_wa_the_new_village_movement = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = GEN_1970_wa_the_new_village_movement NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_GEN_1970_wa_the_new_village_movement } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_GEN_1970_wa_the_new_village_movement } }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_GEN_1970_wa_the_new_village_movement
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_GEN_1970_con_homes_for_the_nation }
		COLDWAR_GRANT_complete_GEN_1970_con_homes_for_the_nation = yes

					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_GEN_1970_capital_defence_scheme
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_GEN_1970_countermeasures_against_aircraft
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_GEN_1970_surface_to_air_missile_belt
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_GEN_1970_integrated_air_defence
				}
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_any_pending
	}
}

COLDWAR_GRANT_complete_LBA_1970_a_revolutionary_administration = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			has_focus_tree = LBA_1970_focus
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_LBA_1970_a_revolutionary_administration }
			tag = LBA
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_LBA_1970_a_revolutionary_administration
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_any_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_GRANT_ready_LBA_1970_a_revolutionary_administration = yes
			}
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus LBA_1970_a_revolutionary_administration"
			LBA_1970_improve_administration = yes
			# IMPERIA COLD WAR — MD fallback build target (preferred state first)
			random_owned_controlled_state = { prioritize = { 391 } 
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						custom_trigger_tooltip = {
							tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_internet_station_1_tt
							check_variable = {
								var = building_level@internet_station
								value = 10
								compare = less_than_or_equals
							}
						}
					}
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
					add_building_construction = {
						type = internet_station
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				treasury_change = -5.00 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_LBA_1970_a_revolutionary_administration
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_LBA_1970_a_revolutionary_administration
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_GRANT_tick_LBA = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_LBA_1970_a_revolutionary_administration }
		COLDWAR_GRANT_complete_LBA_1970_a_revolutionary_administration = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = LBA_1970_a_revolutionary_administration NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_LBA_1970_a_revolutionary_administration } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_LBA_1970_a_revolutionary_administration } }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_done_LBA_1970_a_revolutionary_administration
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_pending_LBA_1970_a_revolutionary_administration
				}
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRANT_any_pending
	}
}
